Let's derive gradient for c_l = ∑_{i in valid_l} h_i(1-h_{i+l}). For l positive (c index? full includes both). In full c array, each l corresponds to both positive and negative? np.correlate index r = lag + n-1. For lag ℓ = r-(n-1). c_ℓ = ∑_{i=max(0,-ℓ)}^{n-1-min?} h_i(1-h_{i+ℓ}). Gradient wrt h_p:

Softmax objective: S= logsumexp(α c) maybe plus max. Gradient weights w_l=exp(α(c_l-cmax))/sum. Then g = ∑ w_l ∂c_l/∂h. We can compute g efficiently using c and weights arrays and convolution-like operations:
For each ℓ (0..n-1), c index r=ℓ+n-1 for positive? Full c symmetric? Not symmetric if h & 1-h? But full includes negatives. Weight for each lag r. We need sum over lags weighted. Gradient at p:
For each r (ℓ=r-(n-1)):
if 0≤p+ℓ<n: g[p] += w_r (1-h[p+ℓ])
if 0≤p-ℓ<n: g[p] -= w_r h[p-ℓ]
This is like convolutions: g = w? Specifically ∑_ℓ w_ℓ (1 - h[p+ℓ]) over valid = ∑_ℓ w_ℓ - ∑ w_ℓ h[p+ℓ]. The first term is sum of weights with shifts for which p in left domain, depends on p and ℓ. The second is generalized overlap: for each p, sum_{ℓ: p+ℓ valid} w_ℓ h[p+ℓ]. That's a Toeplitz weighted cross-correlation. But n=700, we can compute g in O(n^2) by loops over lags with vector slices: for each ℓ, add weights to slices of h. n^2=490k per iteration, fine if few thousands.

Let's check if the discrete optimum may be binary and can be found with simulated annealing. We can do a dedicated binary local search on n=700:
- h binary, sum 350.
- Objective integer max A/B. Need reduce max count. We can maintain c array of counts (float/int). For a pair move flipping one 1 at i and one 0 at j (i != j), update c increments by product of changes? We can compute Δc_m for all m by formulas O(n) per candidate (two single flips sequential O(n) if update). Need search candidates: choose highest mismatch lag ℓ, try flips that reduce A_ℓ or B_ℓ. A_ℓ counts pairs (i,i+ℓ) with 1->0. To reduce A_ℓ, for each such pair, either flip left 1 to 0 or right 0 to1 (but mean fixed requires pair move with another). Pair moves: flip a 1 at i to 0 removes as left in A_ℓ for pairs (i,i+ℓ) if right 0, and affects other lags. Flip a 0 at j to 1 removes as right if left 1. Pseudo:
def pair_feasible_state(V, dlo, dhi, line_zero_max, pos_s, pos_c, neg_s, neg_c, conv_s, conv_c, conc_s, conc_c):
    Ld=dlo; Ud=dhi
    if V + 1e-12 < line_zero_max: return False
    if pos_s.size:
       ub = np.min((V - pos_c)/pos_s)
       if ub < Ud: Ud = ub
       if Ld > Ud + 1e-12: return False
    if neg_s.size:
       lb = np.max((V - neg_c)/neg_s)
       if lb > Ld: Ld = lb
       if Ld > Ud + 1e-12: return False
    ca=1; has_conc=False; ...
    if conv_s.size:
       disc = conv_s*conv_s + 4.0*(V - conv_c)
       if np.any(disc < -1e-12): return False
       disc = np.maximum(disc,0)
       sq = np.sqrt(disc)
       lb = np.max((-conv_s - sq)*0.5)
       ub = np.min((-conv_s + sq)*0.5)
       if lb > Ld: Ld=lb
       if ub < Ud: Ud=ub
       if Ld > Ud + 1e-12: return False
    if has_conc:
       disc = conc_s*conc_s - 8.0*(V - conc_c)
       if disc >= -1e-12:
          if disc <0: disc=0
          sq=np.sqrt(disc)
          a=(conc_s - sq)*0.25
          b=(conc_s + sq)*0.25
          if not (Ld <= a + 1e-12 or Ud >= b - 1e-12): return False
       else:
          a=-np.inf; b=np.inf? no restriction
    return True (and maybe Ld,Ud,a,b)

Let's write a compact state function:

def pair_state(V, dlo, dhi, zmax, ps, pc, ns, nc, cs, cc, hasc, cs0, cc0):
    Ld = dlo; Ud = dhi
    if V + 1e-12 < zmax: return None
    if ps.size:
       ub = float(np.min((V - pc)/ps))
       if ub < Ud: Ud = ub
       if Ld > Ud + 1e-12: return None
    if ns.size:
       lb = float(np.max((V - nc)/ns))
       if lb > Ld: Ld = lb
       if Ld > Ud + 1e-12: return None
    if cs.size:
       disc = cs*cs + 4.0*(V - cc)
       if np.any(disc < -1e-10): return None
       if np.any(disc < 0): disc[disc<0]=0
       sq = np.sqrt(disc)
       lb1 = float(np.max((-cs - sq)*0.5))
       ub1 = float(np.min((-cs + sq)*0.5))
       if lb1 > Ld: Ld = lb1
       if ub1 < Ud: Ud = ub1
       if Ld > Ud + 1e-12: return None
    conc_active = False; a=0.0; b=0.0
    if hasc:
       disc = cs0*cs0 - 8.0*(V - cc0)
       if disc >= -1e-10:
          if disc <0: disc=0
          sq=np.sqrt(disc)
          a=(cs0 - sq)*0.25
          b=(cs0 + sq)*0.25
          if not (Ld <= a + 1e-12 or Ud >= b - 1e-12): return None
          conc_active=True
    return (Ld, Ud, conc_active, a, b)

Then choose_d:
  st=pair_state(V+1e-10, ...)
  if None: return None
  Ld,Ud,ca,a,b=st
  if ca:
     if Ld <= a:
        seg_hi = min(Ud, a)
        if seg_hi < Ld: seg_hi=Ld
        d = 0.5*(Ld+seg_hi)
     else:
        seg_lo = max(Ld, b)
        if seg_lo > Ud: seg_lo=Ud
        d = 0.5*(seg_lo+Ud)
  else:
     # choose 0 if in interval? For reduction, if 0 in interval choose 0 could no improvement. But if V<old, 0 should not satisfy? Actually if V binary lowered, 0 might still be feasible if some other constraints? f(0)=old > V, so at least one line/conv/comp has V<old, so 0 not feasible? For line with c> V and s=0? etc. For line s>0 with c=old and V<old, ub<0, so 0 not. For s<0, lb>0. For convex c=old<V? no. So 0 likely infeasible. Midpoint okay.
     d = 0.5*(Ld+Ud)
  return d

    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full').astype(float)
    best_c = float(c.max())
    improved = False

       if time.time() > deadline: break
       pass_improve = 0.0
       order = range(n) # maybe shuffle for random

          if time.time() > deadline: break
          q = (p + stride) % n

Pseudo:

def try_pair_update(h, c, n, p, q):
    if p == q: return False
    hp=float(h[p]); hq=float(h[q])
    dlo = max(-hp, hq - 1.0)
    dhi = min(1.0 - hp, hq)
    if dhi - dlo <= 1e-13: return False
    sp = n-1-p; if sp<0 sp=0; ep=n-1+p; maxidx=2*n-2; if ep>maxidx ep=maxidx
    sq = n-1-q; ...
    start = min(sp,sq); end=max(ep,eq)
    unaff = 0.0
    if start > 0: um = float(np.max(c[:start])); if um>unaff: unaff=um
    if end < maxidx: um = float(np.max(c[end+1:])); if um>unaff: unaff=um
    g0 = float(np.max(c))
    if unaff >= g0 - 1e-12: return False
    L=end-start+1
    ell = np.arange(start-(n-1), end-(n-1)+1, dtype=np.int64?) np.arange int.
    # a/b
    a = np.zeros(L); b = np.zeros(L)
    idx = p + ell
    valid = (idx >=0)&(idx<n)
    a[valid] += 1.0 - h[idx[valid]]
    idx2 = p - ell
    valid2 = (idx2 >=0)&(idx2<n)
    a[valid2] -= h[idx2[valid2]]
    idx = q + ell ...
    s = a - b
    qcoef = np.zeros(L)
    diff = p-q; if diff<0 diff=-diff
    zero = (ell == 0); if zero.any(): qcoef[zero] = -2.0
    cross = (ell == diff) | (ell == -diff); if diff>0 and cross.any(): qcoef[cross] += 1.0
    ca = c[start:end+1].copy()
    # masks
    s_tol=1e-13
    zmask = np.abs(s) <= s_tol
    if zmask.any(): zmax = float(np.max(ca[zmask]))
    else: zmax = -1e100
    ppos = s > s_tol; ppneg = s < -s_tol
    ps = s[ppos]; pc = ca[ppos]
    ns = s[ppneg]; nc = ca[ppneg]
    ccmask = qcoef > 0; cs = s[ccmask]; cc=ca[ccmask]
    ccnmask = qcoef <0; hasc=ccnmask.any(); if hasc: idxc=int(np.argmax(ccnmask)); cs0=float(s[idxc]); cc0=float(ca[idxc]) else 0,0
    Vlo=unaff; Vhi=g0
    if Vlo >= Vhi - 1e-12: return False
    for it in range(28):
       mid = 0.5*(Vlo+Vhi)
       if pair_state(mid, dlo,dhi,zmax,ps,pc,ns,nc,cs,cc,hasc,cs0,cc0) is not None:
           Vhi=mid
       else: Vlo=mid
    d=choose_d(Vhi+1e-9,...)
    if d is None: return False
    d=max(dlo,min(dhi,d))
    if abs(d)<1e-14: return False
    new_ca = ca + d*s + d*d*qcoef
    # check for tiny negatives? c should nonnegative but due maybe -1e-12. max okay.
    new_max = float(np.max(new_ca))
    if unaff > new_max: new_max=unaff
    if new_max < g0 - 1e-12:
       h[p]=hp+d; h[q]=hq-d
       # clip h tiny
       if h[p] < 0: h[p]=0.0; ... but clipping changes c. Domain should prevent. Use np.clip? If reset, c mismatch. Maybe no clip. To avoid -1e-16, we can if new<0 and >-1e-12 set 0 and recompute? Not.
       c[start:end+1] = new_ca
       return True
    return False
```

Need choose `d` function:
```
def choose_d(V, dlo,dhi, zmax, ps,pc,ns,nc,cs,cc,hasc,cs0,cc0):
    st = pair_state(V, dlo,dhi,...)
    if st is None: return None
    Ld,Ud,conca,ca_,cb_=st
    if conca:
       if Ld <= ca_:
          hi = min(Ud, ca_)
          if hi < Ld: hi=Ld
          d = 0.0
          if d < Ld: d=Ld
          elif d > hi: d=hi
       else:
          lo = max(Ld, cb_)
          if lo > Ud: lo=Ud
          d=0.0
          if d < lo: d=lo
          elif d > Ud: d=Ud
    else:
       d=0.0
       if d < Ld: d=Ld
       elif d > Ud: d=Ud
    return d
```

`pair_cd` implementation:

```
def pair_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline, stride=1, rng=None, do_random=False):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full').astype(float)
    best = float(c.max())
    for pass in range(max_passes):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       improved = False
       if do_random:
          order = list(zip(rng.permutation(n)[::2], rng.permutation(n)[1::2]))? Ensure unique? Simpler:
          perm = rng.permutation(n)
          for i in range(0,n-1,2): p=perm[i]; q=perm[i+1]
       else:
          order_p = rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else range(n)
          for p in order_p: q=(p+stride)%n; if q==p continue
       for p,q:
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          if try_pair_update(h,c,n,p,q): improved=True
       # recompute c to clean drift and update best
       if improved:
          c = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full').astype(float)
          cur = float(c.max())
          if cur < best: best=cur
          # if sum drift: diff = h.sum()-n/2; if abs(diff)>1e-10: project? but pair moved sum. Numeric maybe 1e-12.
          # threshold tiny? Optional
       else:
          break
    c = np.correlate...
    return h, float(c.max())
```

b=(h>0.5).astype(float)
target=n/2
s=b.sum()
if s>target:
  idx=np.where(b==1)[0]
  if idx.size >= s-target:
     flip = idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[:int(s-target)]] # flip ones with smallest h (closest to 0)

  b[flip]=1

```
def project_feasible(x):
   n=len(x); target=n/2.0
   x=np.clip(x,0,1)
   for _ in range(100):
      diff=target-x.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
      if diff>0:
         room=1-x; tot=room.sum()
      else:
         room=x; tot=room.sum()
      if tot<1e-15: break
      # distribute without hitting bounds better: add diff*room/tot? This may overshoot; clip.
      x=np.clip(x + diff*room/tot,0,1)
   # final exact? if not exact, adjust free vars
   return x
```
This preserves approximate. To get exact sum to 1e-13, we can after loop adjust:
```
diff=target-x.sum()
if abs(diff)>1e-13:
   free = (x>1e-12)&(x<1-1e-12)
   if free.sum(): x[free] += diff/free.sum(); x=np.clip(...)
   else: # distribute to non-boundaries? but okay
```
Iterative enough.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-10
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try: h_parent=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n_parent=len(h_parent)
    except: h_parent=np.full(120,0.5); n_parent=120
    h_parent=project_feasible(h_parent)
    best_h=h_parent.copy(); best_n=n_parent; best_c5=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    x_parent=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,best_n)
    # sym candidate
    hs=0.5*(h_parent+h_parent[::-1]); hs=project_feasible(hs)
    cs5=compute_c5(hs)
    if cs5 < best_c5: update
    # small n search budget 180s maybe if n_parent > 80
    if best_n > 80:
       for ntry in [100,140,180,240,300,400]:
          if time.time() > deadline-400: break
          if ntry >= best_n: continue
          x=np.linspace(0,2,ntry)
          hc=np.interp(x,x_parent,best_h)
          hc=project_feasible(hc)
          # also binary
          hb=make_binary_feasible(hc,ntry)
          # maybe random parabolic? no
          for cand in (hc,hb):
             h2=cand.copy()
             if time.time()<deadline-350:
                h2,_=pair_cd(h2,ntry,30,deadline-350,stride=1,rng)
             if time.time()<deadline-300:
                h2,_=pair_cd(h2,ntry,20,deadline-300,stride=max(1,ntry//2),rng)
             c5=compute_c5(h2,ntry)
             if c5<best_c5:
                best_h=h2.copy(); best_n=ntry; best_c5=c5; x_parent=x
          # maybe if best_c5 <=0.38: break? Could continue? break small search to save main
          if best_c5 <= 0.3800: break
    # Main n (probably 700)
    n_main=best_n if best_n>200 else n_parent? If small found better, maybe also optimize main original. We'll keep n_parent separately for main if different.
```

Detailed functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n)), c? 
```
We often need c. Define `corr_max(h)` returns max; `corr(h)` returns c.

```
def correlation(h): return np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full').astype(float)
def c5_from_corr(c,n): return float(np.max(c))*2.0/n
```

project_feasible:
```
def project_feasible(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    n=x.size; target=0.5*n
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(80):
       diff=target-x.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-x; tot=room.sum()
       else:
          room=x; tot=room.sum()
       if tot<1e-15: break
       x=np.clip(x + (diff/tot)*room,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-x.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       free=(x>1e-12)&(x<1.0-1e-12)
       if free.sum()>0:
          x= x.copy(); x[free]+=diff/free.sum(); x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    return x
```
This may not exact after final clip; okay. Could use while more but fine.

make_binary_feasible:
```
def make_binary_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    if abs(target-round(target))>1e-12: return project_feasible(h)
    b=(h>0.5).astype(float)
    s=int(round(b.sum()))
    tgt=int(round(target))
    need=s-tgt
    if need>0:
       idx=np.where(b==1.0)[0]
       if idx.size>0:
          flip=idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[:need]]
          b[flip]=0.0
    elif need<0:
       idx=np.where(b==0.0)[0]
       if idx.size>0:
          flip=idx[np.argsort(-h[idx])[: -need]]
          b[flip]=1.0
    # if still off, fix by flipping nearest
    diff=b.sum()-target
    if abs(diff)>1e-9:
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.where(b==1.0)[0]
          if idx.size: b[idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[:int(np.ceil(diff))]]]=0
       else:
          idx=np.where(b==0.0)[0]
          if idx.size: b[idx[np.argsort(-h[idx])[:int(np.ceil(-diff))]]]=1
    return b
```
For n small; okay.

pair_state: Need pass arrays. It returns None or tuple.

```
def pair_state(V, dlo, dhi, zmax, ps, pc, ns, nc, cs, cc, hasc, cs0, cc0):
    eps=1e-12
    Ld=dlo; Ud=dhi
    if V + 1e-10 < zmax:
       return None
    if ps.size:
       ub=float(np.min((V-pc)/ps))
       if ub < Ud: Ud=ub
       if Ld > Ud + 1e-10: return None
    if ns.size:
       lb=float(np.max((V-nc)/ns))
       if lb > Ld: Ld=lb
       if Ld > Ud + 1e-10: return None
    if cs.size:
       disc=cs*cs + 4.0*(V-cc)
       if np.any(disc < -1e-9): return None
       if np.any(disc < 0.0): disc=np.maximum(disc,0.0)
       sq=np.sqrt(disc)
       lb=float(np.max((-cs - sq)*0.5))
       ub=float(np.min((-cs + sq)*0.5))
       if lb > Ld: Ld=lb
       if ub < Ud: Ud=ub
       if Ld > Ud + 1e-10: return None
    active=False; a=0.0; b=0.0
    if hasc:
       disc=cs0*cs0 - 8.0*(V-cc0)
       if disc >= -1e-9:
          if disc<0.0: disc=0.0
          sq=np.sqrt(disc)
          a=(cs0 - sq)*0.25
          b=(cs0 + sq)*0.25
          if not (Ld <= a + 1e-10 or Ud >= b - 1e-10): return None
          active=True
    return (Ld,Ud,active,a,b)
```
Check `np.maximum(disc,0.0)` returns new; if disc scalar? array. ok.

choose_d:
```
def choose_d(V, dlo, dhi, zmax,...):
    st=pair_state(V,...)
    if st is None: return None
    Ld,Ud,active,ca,cb=st
    if active:
       if Ld <= ca:
          hi = min(Ud, ca)
          if hi < Ld: hi=Ld
          d=0.0
          if d < Ld: d=Ld
          elif d > hi: d=hi
       else:
          lo = max(Ld, cb)
          if lo > Ud: lo=Ud
          d=0.0
          if d < lo: d=lo
          elif d > Ud: d=Ud
    else:
       d=0.0
       if d < Ld: d=Ld
       elif d > Ud: d=Ud
    return d
```

Now pair_cd:
```
def pair_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline, stride=1, rng=None):
    c = correlation(h)
    for _ in range(max_passes):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       improved_passed = False
       if rng is not None:
          order = rng.permutation(n)
       else:
          order = np.arange(n)
       for p in order:
          if time.time() > deadline: break
          q = (p + stride) % n
          if q == p: continue
          if try_pair_update(h,c,n,p,q): improved_passed=True
       if improved_passed:
          # clean drift
          # optionally if sum drift: adjust? pair preserves, but recompute
          c = correlation(h)
       else:
          break
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    return h, cmax
```
If `better = False` after no pass, break. But if improved_passed true but cmax increased due drift? h true max maybe; we continue. We don't track best inside; return h maybe worse than input? It accepts only true pair updates? Since try_pair uses c approximate; could lead to true max slightly higher. We can compare final to original in caller and keep better. To avoid degradation, in pair_cd keep `best_h=h.copy(); best_c5?` It can track cmax true after each pass; if cmax > best? We should return best. Let's implement inside:

  else break
best_h=project? no.
return best_h,best_c
```

def random_pair_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng):
   c=correlation(h); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=...
   for pass:
      improved=False
      perm=rng.permutation(n)
      for i in range(0,n-1,2):
         p=perm[i]; q=perm[i+1]
         if try_pair_update(...): improved=True
      if improved: c=correlation(h); cur=...; if cur<best_c ... else revert
      else break
   return best_h,best_c
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+max(1,budget_s)-5
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        parent=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n_parent=len(parent)
    except Exception:
        parent=np.full(160,0.5); n_parent=160
    parent=project_feasible(parent)
    best_h=parent.copy(); best_n=n_parent; best_c5=compute_c5(best_h)
    # better parent original vs sym
    hsym=0.5*(parent+parent[::-1]); hsym=project_feasible(hsym); csym=compute_c5(hsym)
    if csym < best_c5:
       best_h=hsym; best_c5=csym; parent=hsym; best_n=n_parent
    x_best=np.linspace(0,2,best_n)
    # small n
    if best_n > 100 and time.time() < deadline - 300:
       for ntry in (100, 140, 180, 240, 300, 400):
          if time.time() > deadline - 350: break
          if ntry >= best_n: continue
          x = np.linspace(0,2,ntry)
          hc = project_feasible(np.interp(x, x_best, best_h))
          hb = make_binary_feasible(hc, ntry)
          for cand in hc, hb:
             if time.time() > deadline - 350: break
             h2, cmax = pair_cd(cand.copy(), ntry, 24, deadline - 320, stride=1, rng=rng)
             h2b,cmaxb = pair_cd(h2, ntry, 16, deadline - 300, stride=max(1,ntry//2), rng=rng)
             c5b=compute_c5_from_cmax(cmaxb,ntry)
             if c5b < best_c5:
                best_h=h2b.copy(); best_n=ntry; best_c5=c5b; x_best=np.linspace(0,2,ntry)
             # rounding
             if ntry % 2 ==0:
                hr=make_binary_feasible(h2b,ntry); c5r=compute_c5(hr)
                if c5r < best_c5: update
          if best_c5 <= 0.38: break
```

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(x):
    x=np.clip(np.asarray(x,float),0,1); t=0.5*x.size
    for _ in range(80):
        d=t-x.sum()
        if abs(d)<1e-15: break
        r=1-x if d>0 else x; s=r.sum()
        if s<1e-15: break
        x=np.clip(x+(d/s)*r,0,1)
    d=t-x.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-14:
        f=(x>1e-12)&(x<1-1e-12)
        if f.sum(): x[f]+=d/f.sum(); x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    return x
```

`make_binary_feasible` compact:
```
def make_binary_feasible(h,n):
    t=n/2.0
    if abs(t-round(t))>1e-9: return project_feasible(h)
    b=(h>0.5).astype(float); tgt=int(round(t)); s=int(round(b.sum()))
    k=s-tgt
    if k>0:
       idx=np.where(b==1)[0]
       if idx.size: b[idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[:k]]]=0
    elif k<0:
       idx=np.where(b==0)[0]
       if idx.size: b[idx[np.argsort(-h[idx])[: -k]]]=1
    d=b.sum()-t
    if abs(d)>1e-8:
       if d>0:
          idx=np.where(b==1)[0]; k=int(np.ceil(d))
          if idx.size: b[idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[:k]]]=0
       else:
          idx=np.where(b==0)[0]; k=int(np.ceil(-d))
          if idx.size: b[idx[np.argsort(-h[idx])[:k]]]=1
    return b
```
Potential if k>idx.size (shouldn't), slicing okay. For negative -k int.

`pair_state`: Use local eps. For conv, `if cs.size:`. `disc = cs*cs + 4.0*(V-cc)`. If `np.any(disc < -1e-8)` return None. `if np.any(disc < 0): disc = np.maximum(disc,0.0)`. Then sqrt. Need root1 = (-cs - sq)*0.5; root2 = (-cs + sq)*0.5. Use float max/min.
For conc, `if hasc:`. Good.

`try_pair_update`: Need keep line long but okay.
```
def try_pair_update(h,c,n,p,q):
    if p==q: return False
    hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]
    dlo=max(-hp,hq-1.0); dhi=min(1.0-hp,hq)
    if dhi-dlo<1e-13: return False
    st1,en1=var_range(n,p); st2,en2=var_range(n,q)
    start=st1 if st1<st2 else st2; end=en1 if en1>en2 else en2
    unaff=0.0
    if start>0:
       v=float(np.max(c[:start])); unaff=v if v>unaff else unaff
    if end<2*n-2:
       v=float(np.max(c[end+1:])); unaff=v if v>unaff else unaff
    g0=float(np.max(c))
    if unaff >= g0 - 1e-11: return False
    ell=np.arange(start-(n-1), end-(n-1)+1, dtype=np.int64)
    L=ell.size; a=np.zeros(L); b=np.zeros(L)
    idx=p-ell; m=(idx>=0)&(idx<n); a[m]+=1.0-h[idx[m]]
    idx=p+ell; m=(idx>=0)&(idx<n); a[m]-=h[idx[m]]
    idx=q-ell; m=(idx>=0)&(idx<n); b[m]+=1.0-h[idx[m]]
    idx=q+ell; m=(idx>=0)&(idx<n); b[m]-=h[idx[m]]
    s=a-b; qc=np.zeros(L)
    diff=abs(p-q)
    z=(ell==0); 
    if z.any(): qc[z]-=2.0
    if diff>0:
       cr=(ell==diff)|(ell==-diff)
       if cr.any(): qc[cr]+=1.0
    ca=c[start:end+1]
    tol=1e-13
    zm=np.abs(s)<=tol
    zmax=float(np.max(ca[zm])) if zm.any() else -1e100
    pm=s>tol; nm=s<-tol
    ps=s[pm]; pc=ca[pm]; ns=s[nm]; nc=ca[nm]
    cm=qc>0.5; cs=s[cm]; cc=ca[cm]
    nmq=qc<-0.5
    if nmq.any(): ci=int(np.nonzero(nmq)[0][0]); hasc=True; cs0=float(s[ci]); cc0=float(ca[ci])
    else: hasc=False; cs0=cc0=0.0
    lo=unaff; hi=g0
    for _ in range(PAIR_ITERS):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       if pair_state(mid,dlo,dhi,zmax,ps,pc,ns,nc,cs,cc,hasc,cs0,cc0) is not None: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    V=hi+1e-8
    if V>g0-1e-10: V=g0-1e-10
    d=choose_d(V,dlo,dhi,zmax,ps,pc,ns,nc,cs,cc,hasc,cs0,cc0)
    if d is None: return False
    if d<dlo: d=dlo
    elif d>dhi: d=dhi
    if abs(d)<1e-14: return False
    new=ca+d*s+d*d*qc
    nm=float(np.max(new))
    if unaff>nm: nm=unaff
    if nm < g0 - 1e-11:
       h[p]=hp+d; h[q]=hq-d
       c[start:end+1]=new
       return True
    return False
```
Note `ca=c[start:end+1]` is a view. When we later assign `c[...] = new`, `ca` still view but no use. Good. But when `pm=s>tol`, `pc=ca[pm]` boolean indexing copies, okay. However if after binary we didn't update c, ca still view of c; next time new c slice okay. If we update c, `new` array independent. Good.

Potential problem: `ca` view and `new=ca+d*s+d*d*qc` uses ca before update. fine.

What if `np.max(new)` on view after c part updated? no.

Potential if d makes h[p] slightly outside [0,1] due domain; dlo/dhi exact from hp/hq. But hp/hq from h may be -1e-16; dlo = 1e-16 maybe; okay. We can after update if h[p] >1 and <1+1e-12 set 1. But c update would mismatch. Since domain ensures, skip.

`pair_cd` with best_h copy:
```
def pair_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,stride=1,rng=None):
    c=correlation(h); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=float(np.max(c))
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        improved=False
        order=rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
        for p in order:
            if time.time()>deadline: break
            q=(p+stride)%n
            if q==p: continue
            if 2*stride % n ==0 and p>q: continue
            if try_pair_update(h,c,n,p,q): improved=True
        if improved:
            c=correlation(h); cur=float(np.max(c))
            if cur < best_c:
                best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
            elif cur > best_c + 1e-10:
                h=best_h.copy(); c=correlation(h)
        else: break
    return best_h,best_c
```
If cur > best_c due degradation, revert for subsequent passes. But best_h copy and c recompute. If pair_cd reverts, some previous moves lost; okay. For random similar.

`random_pair_cd`:
```
def random_pair_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng):
   c=correlation(h); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=...
   for _ in range(max_passes):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      improved=False
      perm=rng.permutation(n)
      for i in range(0,n-1,2):
         if time.time()>deadline: break
         p=int(perm[i]); q=int(perm[i+1])
         if p!=q and try_pair_update(h,c,n,p,q): improved=True
      if improved: ...
   return
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+max(1,int(budget_s))-8
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       parent=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); np_=len(parent)
    except Exception:
       parent=np.full(160,0.5); np_=160
    parent=project_feasible(parent)
    best_h=parent.copy(); best_n=np_; best_c5=compute_c5(best_h)
    x_best=np.linspace(0,2,best_n)
    hs=project_feasible(0.5*(parent+parent[::-1]))
    cs=compute_c5(hs)
    if cs<best_c5:
       best_h=hs.copy(); best_c5=cs; parent=hs.copy(); x_best=np.linspace(0,2,best_n)
    parent_h=best_h.copy(); parent_n=best_n
    # small search
    if best_n>100:
      for ntry in (100,140,180,240,320):
        if time.time()>deadline-260: break
        if ntry>=best_n: continue
        x=np.linspace(0,2,ntry)

        cands=[hc, make_binary_feasible(hc,ntry), make_binary_feasible(rng.rand(ntry),ntry)]
        for cand in cands:

          if time.time()<lim-40:

def exact_sum(h):
   t=0.5*len(h)
   d=t-h.sum()
   if abs(d)>0:
      # choose index with most room in direction
      if d>0:
         idx=np.argmax(1-h)
      else: idx=np.argmax(h)
      h[idx]=np.clip(h[idx]+d,0,1)
   return h
```

```
for _ in range(30):
   d=t-x.sum()
   if abs(d)<1e-15: break
   if d>0:
      idx=np.where(x<1-1e-12)[0]
   else: idx=np.where(x>1e-12)[0]
   if idx.size==0: break
   # distribute evenly to idx (with bounds)
   per=d/idx.size
   for i in idx:
      if per>0: x[i]=min(1.0,x[i]+per)
      else: x[i]=max(0.0,x[i]+per)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

PAIR_ITERS = 22

def correlation(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').astype(float)

def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(correlation(h))) * (2.0 / float(n))

def c5_cmax(cm, n):
    return float(cm) * (2.0 / float(n))

def project_feasible(x):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    t = 0.5 * x.size
    for _ in range(80):
        d = t - x.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-15:
            break
        r = 1.0 - x if d > 0 else x
        s = r.sum()
        if s < 1e-15:
            break
        x = np.clip(x + (d / s) * r, 0.0, 1.0)
    d = t - x.sum()
    if abs(d) > 1e-14:
        f = (x > 1e-12) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if f.sum():
            x = x.copy()
            x[f] += d / f.sum()
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x

def make_binary_feasible(h, n):
    t = 0.5 * n
    if abs(t - round(t)) > 1e-9:
        return project_feasible(h)
    b = (h > 0.5).astype(float)
    tgt = int(round(t))
    s = int(round(b.sum()))
    k = s - tgt
    if k > 0:
        idx = np.where(b == 1.0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            b[idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[:min(k, idx.size)]]] = 0.0
    elif k < 0:
        idx = np.where(b == 0.0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            b[idx[np.argsort(-h[idx])[:min(-k, idx.size)]]] = 1.0
    d = b.sum() - t
    if abs(d) > 1e-8:
        if d > 0:
            idx = np.where(b == 1.0)[0]
            k = min(int(np.ceil(d)), idx.size)
            if idx.size and k > 0:
                b[idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[:k]]] = 0.0
        else:
            idx = np.where(b == 0.0)[0]
            k = min(int(np.ceil(-d)), idx.size)
            if idx.size and k > 0:
                b[idx[np.argsort(-h[idx])[:k]]] = 1.0
    return b

def accept_candidate(hh, cc5, bh, bc, bn):
    cc5 = float(cc5)
    if cc5 < float(bc):
        return hh.copy(), cc5, len(hh)
    return bh, bc, bn

def var_range(n, p):
    return (min(p - (n - 1), -p) + (n - 1),
            max(p, (n - 1) - p) + (n - 1))

def pair_state(V, dlo, dhi, zmax, ps, pc, ns, nc, cs, cc, hasc, cs0, cc0):
    Ld, Ud = float(dlo), float(dhi)
    if V + 1e-10 < zmax:
        return None
    if ps.size:
        ub = float(np.min((V - pc) / ps))
        if ub < Ud:
            Ud = ub
        if Ld > Ud + 1e-10:
            return None
    if ns.size:
        lb = float(np.max((V - nc) / ns))
        if lb > Ld:
            Ld = lb
        if Ld > Ud + 1e-10:
            return None
    if cs.size:
        disc = cs * cs + 4.0 * (V - cc)
        if np.any(disc < -1e-9):
            return None
        if np.any(disc < 0.0):
            disc = np.maximum(disc, 0.0)
        sq = np.sqrt(disc)
        lb = float(np.max((-cs - sq) * 0.5))
        ub = float(np.min((-cs + sq) * 0.5))
        if lb > Ld:
            Ld = lb
        if ub < Ud:
            Ud = ub
        if Ld > Ud + 1e-10:
            return None
    active = False
    a = b = 0.0
    if hasc:
        disc = cs0 * cs0 - 8.0 * (V - cc0)
        if disc >= -1e-9:
            if disc < 0.0:
                disc = 0.0
            sq = np.sqrt(disc)
            a = (cs0 - sq) * 0.25
            b = (cs0 + sq) * 0.25
            if not (Ld <= a + 1e-10 or Ud >= b - 1e-10):
                return None
            active = True
    return (Ld, Ud, active, a, b)

def choose_d(V, dlo, dhi, zmax, ps, pc, ns, nc, cs, cc, hasc, cs0, cc0):
    st = pair_state(V, dlo, dhi, zmax, ps, pc, ns, nc, cs, cc, hasc, cs0, cc0)
    if st is None:
        return None
    Ld, Ud, active, a, b = st
    if active:
        if Ld <= a:
            hi = min(Ud, a)
            if hi < Ld:
                hi = Ld
            d = 0.0
            if d < Ld:
                d = Ld
            elif d > hi:
                d = hi
        else:
            lo = max(Ld, b)
            if lo > Ud:
                lo = Ud
            d = 0.0
            if d < lo:
                d = lo
            elif d > Ud:
                d = Ud
    else:
        d = 0.0
        if d < Ld:
            d = Ld
        elif d > Ud:
            d = Ud
    return d

def try_pair_update(h, c, n, p, q):
    if p == q:
        return False
    hp, hq = float(h[p]), float(h[q])
    dlo = max(-hp, hq - 1.0)
    dhi = min(1.0 - hp, hq)
    if dhi - dlo < 1e-13:
        return False

    s1, e1 = var_range(n, p)
    s2, e2 = var_range(n, q)
    start = min(s1, s2)
    end = max(e1, e2)
    maxidx = 2 * n - 2

    unaff = 0.0
    if start > 0:
        v = float(np.max(c[:start]))
        if v > unaff:
            unaff = v
    if end < maxidx:
        v = float(np.max(c[end + 1:]))
        if v > unaff:
            unaff = v

    ca = c[start:end + 1]
    affg = float(np.max(ca))
    g0 = affg if affg > unaff else unaff
    if unaff >= g0 - 1e-11:
        return False

    ell = np.arange(start - (n - 1), end - (n - 1) + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    L = ell.size
    a = np.zeros(L)
    b = np.zeros(L)

    idx = p - ell
    m = (idx >= 0) & (idx < n)
    a[m] += 1.0 - h[idx[m]]
    idx = p + ell
    m = (idx >= 0) & (idx < n)
    a[m] -= h[idx[m]]

    idx = q - ell
    m = (idx >= 0) & (idx < n)
    b[m] += 1.0 - h[idx[m]]
    idx = q + ell
    m = (idx >= 0) & (idx < n)
    b[m] -= h[idx[m]]

    s = a - b
    qc = np.zeros(L)
    diff = abs(p - q)
    z = (ell == 0)
    if z.any():
        qc[z] -= 2.0
    if diff > 0:
        cr = (ell == diff) | (ell == -diff)
        if cr.any():
            qc[cr] += 1.0

    tol = 1e-13
    zm = np.abs(s) <= tol
    zmax = float(np.max(ca[zm])) if zm.any() else -1e100

    pm = s > tol
    nm = s < -tol
    ps = s[pm]
    pc = ca[pm]
    ns = s[nm]
    nc = ca[nm]

    cm = qc > 0.5
    cs = s[cm]
    cc = ca[cm]

    nqm = qc < -0.5
    if nqm.any():
        i0 = int(np.nonzero(nqm)[0][0])
        hasc = True
        cs0 = float(s[i0])
        cc0 = float(ca[i0])
    else:
        hasc = False
        cs0 = 0.0
        cc0 = 0.0

    lo = float(unaff)
    hi = float(g0) + 1e-10
    for _ in range(PAIR_ITERS):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if pair_state(mid, dlo, dhi, zmax, ps, pc, ns, nc, cs, cc, hasc, cs0, cc0) is not None:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid

    vchoose = hi + 1e-8
    if vchoose > g0 - 1e-10:
        vchoose = g0 - 1e-10
    d = choose_d(vchoose, dlo, dhi, zmax, ps, pc, ns, nc, cs, cc, hasc, cs0, cc0)
    if d is None:
        return False
    if d < dlo:
        d = dlo
    elif d > dhi:
        d = dhi
    if abs(d) < 1e-14:
        return False

    new = ca + d * s + d * d * qc
    nm = float(np.max(new))
    if unaff > nm:
        nm = unaff
    if nm < g0 - 1e-11:
        h[p] = hp + d
        h[q] = hq - d
        c[start:end + 1] = new
        return True
    return False

def pair_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline, stride=1, rng=None):
    c = correlation(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(np.max(c))
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        improved = False
        order = rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
        for p in order:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            q = (p + stride) % n
            if q == p:
                continue
            if 2 * stride % n == 0 and p > q:
                continue
            if try_pair_update(h, c, n, int(p), int(q)):
                improved = True
        if improved:
            c = correlation(h)
            cur = float(np.max(c))
            if cur < best_c:
                best_c = cur
                best_h = h.copy()
            elif cur > best_c + 1e-10:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = correlation(h)
        else:
            break
    return best_h, best_c

def random_pair_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng):
    c = correlation(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(np.max(c))
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        improved = False
        perm = rng.permutation(n)
        for i in range(0, n - 1, 2):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            p = int(perm[i])
            q = int(perm[i + 1])
            if p != q and try_pair_update(h, c, n, p, q):
                improved = True
        if improved:
            c = correlation(h)
            cur = float(np.max(c))
            if cur < best_c:
                best_c = cur
                best_h = h.copy()
            elif cur > best_c + 1e-10:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = correlation(h)
        else:
            break
    return best_h, best_c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(1, int(budget_s)) - 8
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n_parent = len(parent)
    except Exception:
        parent = np.full(160, 0.5)
        n_parent = 160

    parent = project_feasible(parent)
    best_h = parent.copy()
    best_n = n_parent
    best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)
    x_best = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, best_n)

    hs = project_feasible(0.5 * (parent + parent[::-1]))
    best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(hs, compute_c5(hs), best_h, best_c5, best_n)
    if len(hs) == best_n:
        pass
    x_best = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, best_n)
    parent_h = best_h.copy()
    parent_n = best_n

    if best_n > 100 and time.time() < deadline - 280:
        for ntry in (100, 140, 180, 240, 320):
            if time.time() > deadline - 250:
                break
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, ntry)
            hc = project_feasible(np.interp(x, x_best, best_h))
            cand_list = [hc, make_binary_feasible(hc, ntry), make_binary_feasible(rng.rand(ntry), ntry)]
            for cand in cand_list:
                if time.time() > deadline - 230:
                    break
                h2 = cand.copy()
                lim = deadline - 180
                h2, c1 = pair_cd(h2, ntry, 30, lim, stride=1, rng=rng)
                if time.time() < lim - 40:
                    h2, c1 = pair_cd(h2, ntry, 18, lim - 40, stride=max(1, ntry // 2), rng=rng)
                if time.time() < deadline - 140:
                    h2, c1 = random_pair_cd(h2, ntry, 22, deadline - 140, rng)
                c5 = c5_cmax(c1, ntry)
                best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(h2, c5, best_h, best_c5, best_n)
                if ntry % 2 == 0:
                    hb = make_binary_feasible(h2, ntry)
                    best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(hb, compute_c5(hb), best_h, best_c5, best_n)
                x_best = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, best_n)
            if best_c5 <= 0.3800:
                break

    n_main = parent_n
    if time.time() < deadline - 180:
        h_main = project_feasible(parent_h.copy())
        h_main, cm = pair_cd(h_main, n_main, 22, deadline - 180, stride=1, rng=rng)
        best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(h_main, c5_cmax(cm, n_main), best_h, best_c5, best_n)

    if n_main % 2 == 0 and time.time() < deadline - 150:
        hb = make_binary_feasible(h_main, n_main)
        best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(hb, compute_c5(hb), best_h, best_c5, best_n)
        ht = np.where(h_main < 1e-3, 0.0, np.where(h_main > 1.0 - 1e-3, 1.0, h_main))
        ht = project_feasible(ht)
        best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(ht, compute_c5(ht), best_h, best_c5, best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 140:
        h2 = project_feasible(best_h.copy()) if best_n == n_main else project_feasible(parent_h.copy())
        h2, cm = pair_cd(h2, n_main, 18, deadline - 140, stride=max(1, n_main // 2), rng=rng)
        best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(h2, c5_cmax(cm, n_main), best_h, best_c5, best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 110:
        h2 = project_feasible(best_h.copy()) if best_n == n_main else project_feasible(parent_h.copy())
        h2, cm = pair_cd(h2, n_main, 15, deadline - 110, stride=max(1, n_main // 3), rng=rng)
        best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(h2, c5_cmax(cm, n_main), best_h, best_c5, best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 90:
        h2 = project_feasible(best_h.copy()) if best_n == n_main else project_feasible(parent_h.copy())
        h2, cm = random_pair_cd(h2, n_main, 18, deadline - 90, rng)
        best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(h2, c5_cmax(cm, n_main), best_h, best_c5, best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 60:
        nb = best_n
        hb0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nb)
        for r in range(3):
            if time.time() > deadline - 35:
                break
            noise = 0.012 * (r + 1) * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, nb)
            hp = project_feasible(best_h + noise)
            hp, cm = pair_cd(hp, nb, 12, deadline - 35, stride=1, rng=rng)
            best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(hp, c5_cmax(cm, nb), best_h, best_c5, best_n)
            if best_n % 2 == 0:
                hb2 = make_binary_feasible(hp, best_n)
                best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(hb2, compute_c5(hb2), best_h, best_c5, best_n)
            if time.time() < deadline - 35:
                hp, cm = random_pair_cd(hp, nb, 10, deadline - 35, rng)
                best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(hp, c5_cmax(cm, nb), best_h, best_c5, best_n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
        hfin = project_feasible(best_h.copy())
        hfin, cm = pair_cd(hfin, best_n, 10, deadline - 3, stride=1, rng=rng)
        best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(hfin, c5_cmax(cm, best_n), best_h, best_c5, best_n)

    h_out = project_feasible(best_h.copy())
    c5_out = compute_c5(h_out)
    best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(h_out, c5_out, best_h, best_c5, best_n)
    if time.time() < deadline - 2:
        h_out, cm = pair_cd(project_feasible(best_h.copy()), best_n, 4, deadline - 1, stride=1, rng=rng)
        best_h, best_c5, best_n = accept_candidate(h_out, c5_cmax(cm, best_n), best_h, best_c5, best_n)

    best_h = np.clip(project_feasible(best_h), 0.0, 1.0)
    return best_h, float(compute_c5(best_h)), int(best_n)
```
